WP No. 26459 of 2010:- Petition filed under Article 226 of The
Constitution of India praying for a Writ of Mandamus directing
the respondent to regularise the service of the petitioner as
Primary School Headmaster from 01.07.1971 and pay the arrears
amount as per G.O. Ms. No.234, Education Department (G2) dated
10.09.2009 within the time frame fixed by this Court.
WP No.26045 of 2014:Petition praying to issue a Writ of
Mandamus directing the respondents herein to extend the
benefits of G.O.Ms.No.234, School Education, dated 10.9.2009 to
the petitioners with effect from the date of promotion, as Head
Master of Elementary School, in the post of Elementary School
Head Master by counting the entire service rendered in the post
https://hcservices.ecourts.gov.in/hcservices/
of Secondary Grade Teacher and confer all consequential benefits

WP No.27643 of 2014,27645,27647 to 27649, 27651/14
28692,28694/14,30271 &30274/14:Petition praying to issue a Writ
of Mandamus directing the respondents herein to award Selection
and Special Grade to the petitioner herein by computing the
entire period of service rendered as Secondary Grade Teacher
prior to 1.6.1988 as was extended to other similarly placed
teachers as per G.O.Ms. No.1381, Education Department, dated
5.10.1990 and G.O.(Ms) No.234, Education (G2) Department, dated
10.9.2009 with all arrears and consequential benefits thereon
WP No.8090 of 2012:Writ of Certiorarified Mandamus to call for
the records relating to the impugned order of he first
respondent in G.O.Ms. No.234, School Education (G-2)
Department, dated 10.9.2009 and quash the same in so far as it
restricts the benefit of pay fixation only to 63 teachers who
had filed Writ Petitions and direct the respondents to extend
the benefit of G.O.Ms.No.234, School Education (G-2)
Department, dated 10.9.2009 to the petitioner also and count
the service rendered by her in the post of Secondary Grade
Teacher prior to 1.6.1988 along with the service rendered in
the post of Primary School Headmaster and accordingly fix her
pay in the selection/special grades of Primary School
Headmaster and grant all consequential benefits to the
petitioner.
WP No.29288 of 2014,30370,30371/14,30391/14, 30392/14,
30395/14, 30411/14, 30418/14:Petition praying to issue a Writ
of Certiorarified Mandamus, Calling for the entire records
pertaining to the orders passed by the, the 3rd respondent
herein vide his proceedings No.Na.Ka. No.840/A5/2014 dated
29.9.2014 and quash them as illegal, arbitrary, unreasonable
being violative of rules and principles of natural justice, and
thereby direct the respondents to extend the benefit of the
G.O.Ms. No.234 School Education (G2) Department dated 10.9.2009
and accordingly to award Selection Grade /Special Grade Scale
of Pay in the post of Elementary School Headmaster by taking in
to account of their lower cadre Secondary Grade Teacher service
as was given to 63 persons is concerned.

School Headmistress by extending the benefits of G.O.No.234,
School Education (G2) Department, dated 10.09.2009 in the light
of Division Bench Common Order dated 07.07.2011 in W.A.815/2010
and Government Letter No. 29949/EE(1)2/2014-21 dated 13.03.2014
and Letter No. 29949/EE(1)2/2014-17 dated 25.4.2014 by counting
the service rendered by the petitioner before 01.06.1988 as
Secondary Grade Teacher and Elementary School Headmaster to the
petitioner wife and Consequently fix the family pension paid to
the petitioner.
